# Knob Registry

Every flag, key, argument, and constant that changes what Clio Coder does, rendered from `docs/knobs.yaml` by `npm run knobs`. The `knob-registry` check in `scripts/check-hygiene.ts` (run by `npm run lint`) fails when the source tree reads a knob the registry does not list, when the registry lists one the tree no longer reads, when a settings or constant default disagrees with the code, or when this page is stale. Declare a new knob in the registry first; the check tells you which surface it found it on.

Columns: **Default** is what applies when nothing sets the knob. **Read at** cites the source line the check verified. **Doc** lists other pages that mention the knob (none means the registry row is its only documentation). **Test** lists test files that mention it. **Verdict** is the maintainers' call: `keep`, `merge` into another knob, `deprecate` behind a warning, `document` (kept, and this row is now its documentation), or `remove`.

| Settings keys (settings.yaml) | 158 | 16 | 113 | 142 | 0 | 0 | 16 | 0 |
| CLI flags | 321 | 13 | 214 | 304 | 0 | 11 | 6 | 0 |

| Tool arguments that carry policy | 97 | 8 | 63 | 89 | 0 | 0 | 8 | 0 |
| Agent recipe frontmatter | 22 | 0 | 6 | 22 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| Prompt fragment frontmatter | 4 | 0 | 0 | 4 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| Model knowledge-base keys | 82 | 31 | 74 | 51 | 0 | 0 | 31 | 0 |

| **Total** | **921** | **168** | **667** | **747** | **0** | **13** | **161** | **0** |

## Environment variables

Read from `process.env` under `src/`. Durable policy lives in settings; these override one process, mark Clio's own children, or exist for tests. `docs/environment-variables.md` carries the prose.

## Settings keys (settings.yaml)

Every dotted key the settings-v2 schema accepts. `<key>` stands for an operator-chosen name and `[]` for a list element. Layering: built-in default, then the user `settings.yaml`, then `.clio-coder/settings.yaml`, then `.clio-coder/settings.local.yaml`, then CLI flags for one process.

| Name | Owner | Default | Controls | Precedence | Read at | Doc | Test | Verdict |

## CLI flags

Every `--flag` a command parser under `src/cli` matches. `global` flags come before the subcommand. A flag overrides the setting it names for one process and never writes it.

### `clio-coder`

| Name | Owner | Default | Controls | Precedence | Read at | Doc | Test | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `--acp` | global |  | Startup alias for the `acp` subcommand: rewrites the rest of the line onto `clio-coder acp`, so every trailing option is parsed by the ACP parser. |  | `src/cli/argv.ts:169` | `docs/acp.md` |  | keep |
| `--all` | global |  | With `--help`, appends the `clio-coder dev` command list (components, evolve, share) to the top-level help; no effect otherwise. |  | `src/cli/argv.ts:100`, `src/cli/index.ts:167` | `docs/built-in-agents.md`, `docs/commands-and-modes.md`, `docs/configuration-and-targets.md`, `docs/context-engine.md`, `docs/extensions-and-sharing.md`, `docs/installation-and-lifecycle.md` |  | keep |
| `--api-key` | global |  | Startup flag taking a literal key that overrides the stored credential for the target this invocation resolves; must precede the subcommand. | `--api-key` > credentials.yaml entry and the `--api-key-env` variable for the resolved target, this process only | `src/cli/argv.ts:108`, `src/cli/argv.ts:195`, `src/cli/argv.ts:208` | `docs/commands-and-modes.md`, `docs/configuration-and-targets.md`, `docs/exit-codes-and-output.md`, `docs/troubleshooting.md` | `contracts/auth-login-write-failure.test.ts`, `contracts/auth-storage-durability.test.ts` | keep |
| `--continue` | global |  | Refused before the subcommand: exits 2 with the hint that sessions are resumed from inside the app by typing /resume; nothing is parsed from it. `run --continue` is a separate flag. |  | `src/cli/argv.ts:142` | `docs/commands-and-modes.md`, `docs/documentation-guide.md`, `docs/session-lifecycle.md` |  | keep |

### `clio-coder acp`

| Name | Owner | Default | Controls | Precedence | Read at | Doc | Test | Verdict |

## Model knowledge-base keys

Key paths in the model knowledge base (`src/domains/providers/models/**/*.yaml`). `<gpuTiers>`-style segments are data. Only `capabilities`, `quirks.kvCache`, `quirks.sampling`, and `quirks.thinking` reach a request; the rest is provenance or server-start advice.

| Name | Owner | Default | Controls | Precedence | Read at | Doc | Test | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `capabilities` | providers |  | Object of `CapabilityFlags` a knowledge-base entry contributes; it is merged over runtime defaults and under target overrides to build the synthesized model. |  | `src/domains/providers/models/cloud-models/alcf.yaml:10`, `src/domains/providers/models/local-models/clio-coder-local-coding-targets.yaml:57` | `docs/README.md`, `docs/acp.md`, `docs/built-in-agents.md`, `docs/commands-and-modes.md`, `docs/configuration-and-targets.md`, `docs/documentation-guide.md`, `docs/model-catalog.md`, `docs/prompt-envelope-and-tools.md`, `docs/provider-adapter-cookbook.md`, `docs/safety-model.md` | `boundaries/check-boundaries.ts`, `contracts/worker-boundary.test.ts`, `harness/dispatch-stub-context.ts`, `harness/openai-compat-fixture.ts`, `smoke/acp-boundary.test.ts`, `smoke/process-lifecycle.test.ts` | keep |
| `capabilities.audio` | providers |  | Boolean audio-input flag carried into CapabilityFlags for badges and dashboards; no request builder reads it. |  | `src/domains/providers/models/cloud-models/alcf.yaml:17`, `src/domains/providers/models/local-models/clio-coder-local-coding-targets.yaml:65` | `docs/acp.md`, `docs/configuration-and-targets.md` |  | keep |
| `capabilities.chat` | providers |  | Boolean saying the model serves chat; feeds runtime resolution and the `C` badge in `targets` and `models` output. |  | `src/domains/providers/models/cloud-models/alcf.yaml:11`, `src/domains/providers/models/local-models/clio-coder-local-coding-targets.yaml:58` | `docs/acp.md`, `docs/architecture.md`, `docs/commands-and-modes.md`, `docs/config-knobs-audit.md`, `docs/configuration-and-targets.md`, `docs/context-engine.md`, `docs/documentation-guide.md`, `docs/environment-variables.md`, `docs/model-catalog.md`, `docs/observability.md`, `docs/pi-boundary.md`, `docs/proactive-memory.md`, `docs/prompt-envelope-and-tools.md`, `docs/provider-adapter-cookbook.md`, `docs/release-notes/v0.3.9-local-economics.md`, `docs/release-notes/v0.3.9-progress.md`, `docs/release-notes/v0.3.9-release-test.md`, `docs/tui-design.md` | `boundaries/check-boundaries.ts`, `harness/openai-compat-fixture.ts` | keep |
| `capabilities.contextWindow` | providers |  | Token context window of the synthesized model unless a probe or configured target value overrides it; drives compaction pressure and context-floor warnings. |  | `src/domains/providers/models/cloud-models/alcf.yaml:21`, `src/domains/providers/models/local-models/clio-coder-local-coding-targets.yaml:69` | `docs/configuration-and-targets.md`, `docs/context-working-set.md`, `docs/prompt-envelope-and-tools.md` |  | keep |
| `capabilities.embeddings` | providers |  | Boolean embeddings flag carried into CapabilityFlags for the `E` badge and dashboards; no request builder reads it. |  | `src/domains/providers/models/cloud-models/alcf.yaml:18`, `src/domains/providers/models/local-models/clio-coder-local-coding-targets.yaml:66` | `docs/configuration-and-targets.md` |  | keep |
| `capabilities.fim` | providers |  | Boolean fill-in-the-middle flag for the `F` badge and dashboards; no request builder reads it. |  | `src/domains/providers/models/cloud-models/alcf.yaml:20`, `src/domains/providers/models/local-models/clio-coder-local-coding-targets.yaml:68` | `docs/configuration-and-targets.md` |  | keep |
| `capabilities.maxTokens` | providers |  | Default max output tokens of the synthesized model unless a probe or target override supplies one; bounds per-turn output and Anthropic-style thinking budgets. |  | `src/domains/providers/models/cloud-models/alcf.yaml:22`, `src/domains/providers/models/local-models/clio-coder-local-coding-targets.yaml:70` | `docs/configuration-and-targets.md`, `docs/proactive-memory.md` |  | keep |
| `capabilities.reasoning` | providers |  | Boolean that marks the synthesized model as reasoning-capable and, with `quirks.thinking.mechanism`, decides whether thinking fields are sent at all. |  | `src/domains/providers/models/cloud-models/alcf.yaml:14`, `src/domains/providers/models/local-models/clio-coder-local-coding-targets.yaml:61` | `docs/acp.md`, `docs/commands-and-modes.md`, `docs/configuration-and-targets.md`, `docs/model-catalog.md`, `docs/pi-boundary.md`, `docs/provider-adapter-cookbook.md`, `docs/release-notes/v0.3.9-local-economics.md`, `docs/tui-design.md` |  | keep |
| `capabilities.rerank` | providers |  | Boolean rerank flag for the `K` badge and dashboards; no request builder reads it. |  | `src/domains/providers/models/cloud-models/alcf.yaml:19`, `src/domains/providers/models/local-models/clio-coder-local-coding-targets.yaml:67` | `docs/configuration-and-targets.md` |  | keep |
| `capabilities.structuredOutputs` | providers |  | Structured-output mode (`json-schema`, `gbnf`, `xgrammar`, `none`); only `json-schema` lets a dispatch `responseSchema` be enforced on the worker request. |  | `src/domains/providers/models/cloud-models/alcf.yaml:15`, `src/domains/providers/models/local-models/clio-coder-local-coding-targets.yaml:63` | `docs/configuration-and-targets.md` |  | keep |

| `capabilities.toolCallFormat` | providers |  | Declared tool-call dialect (`openai`, `qwen`, `hermes`, ...) validated on targets and worker specs; no request builder currently branches on it. |  | `src/domains/providers/models/cloud-models/alcf.yaml:13`, `src/domains/providers/models/local-models/clio-coder-local-coding-targets.yaml:60` | `docs/configuration-and-targets.md` |  | keep |

| `family` | providers |  | Canonical family id; a pattern equal to it counts as a `family` match, and the id lands on `model.clioCoder.family`, where `openai-gpt-oss` selects Harmony handling. |  | `src/domains/providers/models/cloud-models/alcf.yaml:1`, `src/domains/providers/models/local-models/clio-coder-local-coding-targets.yaml:1` | `docs/config-knobs-audit.md`, `docs/configuration-and-targets.md`, `docs/context-engine.md`, `docs/environment-variables.md`, `docs/eval-runner.md`, `docs/model-catalog.md`, `docs/provider-adapter-cookbook.md`, `docs/release-notes/v0.3.9-local-economics.md`, `docs/safety-model.md`, `docs/tool-usage.md`, `docs/troubleshooting.md`, `README.md` | `contracts/extension-reload-slash.test.ts`, `contracts/gemma-channel-filter.test.ts` | keep |
| `matchPatterns` | providers |  | Lowercase substrings matched against the wire model id; the longest matching pattern across the whole knowledge base selects the entry. |  | `src/domains/providers/models/cloud-models/alcf.yaml:7`, `src/domains/providers/models/local-models/clio-coder-local-coding-targets.yaml:52` | `docs/model-catalog.md` |  | keep |

## Hardcoded constants

Numeric constants in the files listed under `constantScopes` in the registry. They are not operator-tunable; they are here because they bound prompts, budgets, and repairs, and a change to one is a behavior change.

| `CLIO_MIN_CONTEXT_WINDOW` | core | `131072` | Context window Clio assumes when a chat target does not report a real one. |  | `src/core/context-floor.ts:19` |  |  | document |
| `CLIO_MIN_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS` | core | `32768` | Output tokens Clio requests per turn when nothing smaller is configured. |  | `src/core/context-floor.ts:22` |  |  | document |
